03:15:35 --> 03:15:40
was like some meaning written upon the

03:15:37 --> 03:15:41
Stars where you could like go out to the

03:15:39 --> 03:15:43
star where that meaning was written and

03:15:40 --> 03:15:44
like change it around and thereby

03:15:42 --> 03:15:47
completely change the meaning of life

03:15:43 --> 03:15:49
right like like the the notion that this

03:15:46 --> 03:15:50
written on a stone tablet somewhere

03:15:48 --> 03:15:52
implies you could like change the tablet

03:15:49 --> 03:15:54
and get a different meaning and that

03:15:51 --> 03:15:57
seems kind of wacky doesn't it

03:15:53 --> 03:15:59
so it's it's it doesn't feel that

03:15:56 --> 03:16:03
mysterious to me at this point it's just

03:15:58 --> 03:16:06
a matter of being like yeah I care

03:16:05 --> 03:16:12
and part of that is uh

03:16:09 --> 03:16:14
part of that is the love that connects

03:16:11 --> 03:16:17
all of us it's one of the things that I

03:16:13 --> 03:16:17
care about

03:16:17 --> 03:16:22
and the flourishing of the collective

03:16:19 --> 03:16:24
intelligence of the human species

03:16:21 --> 03:16:28
you know that sounds kind of too fancy

03:16:23 --> 03:16:31
to me I just look at all the all the

03:16:27 --> 03:16:34
people you know like one by one up to

03:16:30 --> 03:16:38
the 8 billion and be like that's life

03:16:33 --> 03:16:38
that's life

03:16:37 --> 03:16:44
you're an incredible human it's a huge

03:16:40 --> 03:16:45
honor I was uh trying to talk to you for

03:16:43 --> 03:16:48
a long time

03:16:44 --> 03:16:49
because I'm a big fan I think you're a

03:16:47 --> 03:16:50
really important voice and really

03:16:48 --> 03:16:52
important mind thank you for the fight

03:16:49 --> 03:16:54
you're fighting

03:16:51 --> 03:16:56
um thank you for being fearless and bold

03:16:53 --> 03:16:58
and for everything you do I hope we get

03:16:55 --> 03:16:59
a chance to talk again and I hope you

03:16:57 --> 03:17:00
never give up

thank you for talking today you're

03:16:59 --> 03:17:04
welcome I do worry that we

03:17:03 --> 03:17:07
undamental questions I expect people

03:17:04 --> 03:17:10
have but you know maybe

03:17:06 --> 03:17:13
we got a little bit further and made a

03:17:09 --> 03:17:15
tiny little bit of progress and uh

03:17:12 --> 03:17:16
I'd say like be satisfied with that but

03:17:14 --> 03:17:18
actually no I think one should only be

03:17:15 --> 03:17:19
satisfied with solving the entire

03:17:17 --> 03:17:22
problem
